`talentSpymasterReportCounts`, `talentMerchantPrinceTripInterval`, `talentSageOfLettersEnchanterLevel`,
`talentWardenOfWallsAlarmRadius`.

**Fixture heroes.** The eight slice heroes are all epic, and none uses a bow, a crossbow or v1 summons, so four small
test-only definitions cover the rest of the engine: `fixture_rare` (2 phases plus an authored third, there at level 1),
`fixture_legendary` (`rebirth`), `fixture_bow` (marksman with a real bow: `NpcRangedAttackGoal`, arrows, the
`target_drawing_bow` reaction) and `fixture_summoner` (v1 `summon` of wolves, adds counted against the caps). They are
JSON strings inside the GameTest classes, registered for the test's duration with `HeroRegistry.registerTransient`
(the `GameTestBases.register` pattern), never shipped as content, and drawn with the `_fallback` model. GameTests:
`rareHeroAuthoredThirdPhaseAtLevelOne`, `legendaryRebirthStartsFinalPhase`, `bowHeroShootsRealArrows`,
`summonerHeroSummonsWithinCaps`.

These 61 (41 in the two lists above, 12 talent tests, 4 fixture tests and the 4 static ones) come on top of the v1
ability GameTests (v1 §31.2, about 26, built in A3 and A4): about 87 new GameTests. The mock player is a
`FakePlayer` in survival mode; wherever a test names "an NPC soldier and a mock player", both runs must give the
same numbers, which is the test of equal treatment.

### 14.6 Campaign verification

Milestone 1: the two 8-day slice runs of SLICE_PLAN.md section 6, plus an old-world load. Each content batch: the
3-day smoke of 14.4. Before the final release: a 30-day soak with 4 leaders and 12 more heroes (ROADMAP phase 1's soak
item, now with heroes).

### 14.7 Performance measurements

Section 11: tick time with and without heroes in every campaign check; the render statistics property and lineups of
1, 16 and 64 heroes on the Xvfb client after the slice and after batches 4 and 8; heap after a resource reload once all
200 are in; jar size in every release.

## 15. Work breakdown

Sizes: **S** under half an agent session, **M** about one session, **L** one to two sessions (split if it grows). Every
task ends with its tests green and a reviewer pass (the owner's workflow: implement, review, fix). IDs: A engine, F
format and pure logic, G art generator, L leadership, C content, V verification.

Done 2026-11-13 ("F5 as built" below); reviewed 2026-11-13 ("Review of F1-F5 and A1b" below) |

Kit sheets (C1-C4): each hero also gets its kit sheet `docs/heroes-v2/kits/<id>.txt`, sent to the owner whenever
the hero is ready or its kit changes; from C1 on herogen writes it from the hero source.

Order: A0, F1, F4, G1 and L1 can start at once (L1 does not need GeckoLib). The critical path is F1 -> F2 -> A1 -> A3
-> A5 -> A6 -> V1 -> V3 (six L tasks in a row before the campaign runs; A0 is small and runs beside F1), with G1 -> G3
-> G4 -> G5, G1 -> G6a-c and then C1-C4 alongside. Mobility and work mastery (F5, A10-A12, G10, L5m) are in this
milestone because the slice must prove them before batches are authored and its heroes use them (Ambrose flies); A10
and A11 need only A1 and run beside A3-A6, G10 beside G5, L5m beside L5, so only A12 (after A6, before V1) lengthens
the critical path (`MOBILITY_AND_WORK.md` 18.1).

Plan change (2026-11-12, the implementation workflow): A1 is split into A1a (the entity and its visual side, which needs
only A0) and A1b (the kit-backed parts, after F1 and F2), so the test rig can render in game before the format work
lands. Checkpoint 1 ("pipeline alive": the test rig renders in game, blinks and walks) is reached with A0 + A1a + A2.
Elsewhere in this section, "A1" as a dependency means both halves.

A1a as built (2026-11-12), where it goes beyond or differs from the rows above:
- **A bind-pose renderer already.** A client needs a renderer for every entity type, so A1a registers a minimal
  `client/hero/HeroRenderer` (GeckoLib's `GeoEntityRenderer` at the visual's `render_scale`) and `HeroModel` (geometry,
  texture and the animation chain with its fallbacks from the registry; `_fallback` for fixtures and unknown heroes).
  `HeroEntity.registerControllers` is empty, so the hero stands in its bind pose; A2 adds the controllers, the face,
  the layers, bone visibility (the phase-2 halo still shows), the sash tint and LOD to these two classes.
- **`HeroActions` came with A1a** (listed under F3): the bit layouts of `DATA_ACTION`, `DATA_STATE`, `DATA_MOOD` and
  `DATA_LOCO` and the appendix D code table, with `HeroActionsTest`; F3 adds nothing to it unless the table grows.
- **Registry.** `HeroRegistry` reads `assets/factionclash/heroes/*.visual.json` from the mod file itself (a dedicated
  server loads no client assets, yet sizes heroes), lazily and again at every server start; a config override
  (`config/factionclash/heroes/<id>.json`) may set `name` and `title` in this build (other fields are reported as not
  read yet; `id`, `art` and `animations` never). A hero has the id's name ("Test Rig") until A1b reads definitions.
- **Size.** Hitbox as 3.6 with the PROPOSED 1.2-block floor (the eyes stay on the model) and the 1.98 cap (eyes in
  proportion), width 0.6 x scale; `getDefaultDimensions` divides by `generic.scale` so vanilla's own scaling lands on
  the capped size.
- **Kit.** Until kits exist, the kit is the role's basic weapon (stone sword or axe, bow and arrow, crossbow), bound
  with a custom-data marker: never dropped, never deposited, gone when storehouse gear replaces it, given again on return.
- **Life cycle.** `give` releases the hero from any faction first and spawns a new entity (new UUID) at the operator,
  a given position, or the base's spawn point; revival also makes a new entity and replaces the roster entry, so any
  stale copy of an older entity is discarded when it loads (`HeroState` keeps the last UUID after a release). A new
  campaign frees every hero. `heroes.reviveSeconds` (600) is the first key of the `[heroes]` config section.
- **Director.** A hero's roster entry carries `heroId`; it counts in every soldier count (needs, garrison, war council
  strength) but the raid party is picked from the non-heroes only, and `retrainIdleWorker` skips it.

A2 as built (2026-11-12), where it goes beyond or differs from 4.4-4.9 and the row above:
- **Controllers in common code.** `HeroEntity.registerControllers` hands over to `hero/HeroAnimator`, which builds
  `base` (4 ticks), `action` (2) and `secondary` (6) with the blends of the timing files (`HeroClips.BASE_TRANSITION`
  etc.; `HeroTimingsTest` checks every shipped timing file against them). It touches no client class: what a hero's
  chain holds is asked of a `Library` the client model installs. Each controller picks the first clip of a candidate
  list its chain has (`logic/hero/HeroClips`), so clips the libraries lack fall back (no swim clip: walk; no
  `work.mine`: `attack.1`). `DATA_LOCO` modes come first in the base list (`move.fly`, `move.glide`, `move.climb` ...)
  and fall back to the ground rules, so A10 only has to set the byte.
- **Walk and run speed (PROPOSED, by eye).** An NPC walks at a player's 4.3 blocks/s, more than G3's stroll covers, so
  `move.walk` plays at ground speed / 0.11 blocks per tick (about 2x for a walking NPC) and `move.run` (from 0.245
  blocks/tick, a sprint) at ground speed / 0.20, both clamped to 0.6-2.2 in steps of 0.1 and changed only by 0.3 or
  more (GeckoLib rescales the whole clip clock, which jumps the phase). The feet still slide a little at a walk.
- **Actions.** A new `DATA_ACTION` sequence restarts the action controller (`forceAnimationReset`, the packed speed);
  `name` then `name.loop` when the chain has one. The server clears the code to 0 when the clip has played
  (`HeroTimingFiles`: the timing file's total ticks divided by the speed; loops and channels are never cleared), so a
  player who starts tracking later does not replay an old action. While no action plays, a fresh vanilla swing plays
  the held tool's work clip (`work.mine`, `work.chop`, `work.till`, else `attack.1`): basic attacks show the katana
  cut, but the damage still lands at the swing's start until the move runner (A5) times basic attacks. Death plays
  `death` and holds. Sound keyframes play at the hero on the client; `vfx:` markers are only logged until `HeroVfx`.
- **Marker log and the timing check.** `-Dfactionclash.heroMarkers=true` (set for the `clientViewer` run) logs every
  marker with its animation tick and the game ticks since the action started. In the Xvfb viewer (software GL, about 10
  frames a second) `attack.1`'s `hit` fired at animation tick 10.36-10.72 (file: 10.00), 11.1-12.1 game ticks after the
  data arrived at 1x and 6.9 at 2x: GeckoLib starts the clip on the first frame after the blend and fires an event on
  the first frame past its tick, so each adds up to one frame; at 60 frames a second that is about 0.3 tick.
- **The face** (`client/hero/HeroFace` on `FaceRules`, `BlinkSchedule`, `FaceClock`): the 4.6 priority with the
  distance freeze applied right after "dead"; the mood byte's glowing eyes (and a phase look's `eyes_glow`) replace an
  open-type eye with frame 10, its blush adds cheek frame 1 when the mood has none; a blink in progress opens at once
  when the wind-up bit comes on and an overdue blink waits 6 ticks after it; the first mood seen is not a change for
  the 10-tick hold. The mood table is the visual file's resolved `face.moods` with `face.overrides` on top.
- **A first server mood stack** (`logic/hero/MoodRules`): an operator's forced mood, the hurt wince (vanilla's 10-tick
  hurt time), asleep, focused in a wind-up, determined while fighting, tired below 30% health, otherwise calm. Phase,
  reaction, situation and temperament moods slot in with A6 and L3/L4. The weapon-drawn flag is set while the hero
  has a target and for 5 s (100 ticks) after its last blow.
- **Renderer.** The UV shift is applied in `createVerticesOfQuad` while the `eyes`, `mouth` and `cheeks` cubes are
  drawn, divided by the baked model's texture size; tint bones are the visual file's `tint` list plus any
  `fine_sash*` bone, drawn with the faction's `rgb` as the vertex colour (a hero with no faction does not exist yet;
  previews take one). The glow layer is GeckoLib's `AutoGlowingGeoLayer` through the same path, skipped when the
  current texture has no `_glowmask` file and, for a mask that glows only in the glowing-eye frame, whenever that frame
  is not shown. The item layer draws vanilla items at `item_r`/`item_l` at vanilla size (scaled back by 1 /
  `render_scale`) with vanilla's third-person transforms; a hero whose model has its own weapon never shows a held
  weapon or its kit item and shows no item while the weapon is drawn; a fists-style hero or the neutral model shows
  what it holds (on a chibi a vanilla sword looks large; an open item for the art review).
- **Procedural touches, and a GeckoLib trap.** Head look (pitch +-60, yaw +-80), a 7-degree hit flinch on `chest`
  (none under super armour), ears back 25 and out 15 degrees while hurt or angry, and the sway of the visual file's
  parent-to-child chains against the body's turn (`Sway`: the G3 spring numbers; hanging chains swing on z, others turn
  on y; loose groups such as the two ears are left to the bake). GeckoLib's bone setters mark a bone as changed and its
  next pass then leaves that bone where it is, so offsets added after the pass would add up frame after frame on a bone
  no animation keys (found in the viewer: heads that slowly turned round); `setCustomAnimations` therefore clears the
  markers after its offsets. Cape lift from velocity is left to the baked follow-through.
- **Visibility and level of detail** (`logic/hero/HeroLod`): `fine_` bones within 20 blocks, the face within 16 (the
  cheeks plane hides with it), the secondary controller and the sway within 32, locator tracking within 24, each with
  a one-block band so a hero at a boundary does not flicker. Phase bones follow 4.3 (`p1_` only in phase 1), the
  visual file's phase looks show and hide their lists in phase order, the weapon bones follow the weapon flag, and the
  naming rules also cover bones a visual file forgot to list. Bone visibility is set on every frame.
- **Fallback chain.** Geometry missing: the `_fallback` model, texture and chain. Animation files missing from the
  chain are dropped (each logged once) so GeckoLib never looks a name up in a missing file; the `_p2` texture is used
  only when the file exists.
- **Operator screenshot tools** (`/fc admin hero ...`): `preview <id> [faction] [pos] [yaw]` stands a display copy
  (no AI, cannot be hurt or targeted, no hunger, job or roster entry, never counted by the director or the alarms, kept
  across restarts, named "(preview)"); `lineup [first] [count] [spacing] [faction]` stands a row of them 4 blocks in
  front of the source, facing it (from RCON: `execute as <player> at <player> run fc admin hero lineup 0 7 1.5`);
  `preview clear [id]` / `lineup clear`; `pose <id|all> mood <mood|auto> | action <name> [speed] | stop | base
  <idle|combat|walk|run|swim|fall|crouch|sit|sleep|climb|fly|glide|auto> | weapon <drawn|sheathed|auto> | phase <1-4> |
  talk|windup|blush|glow <on|off> | clear` on every loaded entity of that hero. A forced base pose needs one more
  synced byte, `DATA_POSE`, beside the five of 10.1 (zero except on a hero posed by hand).
- **The six style fixtures without shipping them.** A server reads `config/factionclash/hero_fixtures/<id>.visual.json`
  at every start as preview fixtures (they can be lined up and posed, never given to a faction, so never drafted); a
  client registers the visual files a resource pack adds for ids the mod does not ship (`ClientHeroVisuals`), so the
  pack's geometry, textures and face tables draw them. For the screenshots the fixtures were built with the art
  pipeline's own `build.build_files` / `build.write_files` into a temporary folder (nothing in `tools/herogen` changed),
  their visual files copied to the server's `config/factionclash/hero_fixtures/` and the folder packed as a resource
  pack in the viewer's `run/resourcepacks/`. Maren names `_style_sword_shield`, which does not exist yet: her chain
  drops it.
- **Measured (Xvfb, software GL, a loaded machine):** 0.84-2.3 ms per hero render (the glow layer redraws the whole
  test rig because its mask glows outside the face); the 0.1-0.25 ms estimate of section 11 is for a real GPU and is
  still to be measured there (V2).

Checkpoint 1 in game (2026-11-12; screenshots and GIFs in `docs/heroes-v2/previews/game/cp1/`). The test rig and the
six style fixtures (built from the current `herosrc/style` with the pipeline's own `build_files`/`write_files` into a
scratch folder, as in A2) were checked on a dedicated server from `tools/setup_server.sh` (the A1a world) with the Xvfb
`clientViewer` (window 1280x720, FOV 70, `fovEffectScale` 0, view bobbing off), on a grass stage at y 120:
- **Seen working:** the lineup front and back; walk and run (forced in place, `pose base walk|run`); the katana swing
  at 1x and 2x; blinks (half, closed, closed, half; three in 16 s); all 12 moods; the talking mouth; the sash in four
  faction colours; a pickaxe, axe, hoe, rod, shovel (on the chibi Tansy), torch and shield in the hands; the `fine_` bones
  dropping between 19.5 and 21.6 blocks and back at 19; no vanishing at the screen edge (the hero stays drawn while
  any part of it is on screen); the campaign hero's death (the `death` clip lies flat, no sideways roll) and its revival
  as a new entity (one campaign entity throughout); after `save-all` and a restart the campaign hero and the preview
  copies came back with the same UUIDs, kit and record. No z-fighting on the face planes up close at 45 degrees; the eyes
  sit where the previews put them.
- **Hit marker:** the server now logs a played action's timing-file hits with `-Dfactionclash.heroMarkers=true`
  (`[Heroes] <id> server <clip> hit at +N ticks`): `attack.1` hits at +10 at 1x and +5 at 2x, as agreed. The client's
  marker fired 10.5-12.5 game ticks after the client saw the action at 1x and 6.4-7.0 at 2x, with the viewer at 5-12
  frames a second (1.7-4 ticks a frame); recheck within one tick at 60 fps on a real GPU (A5, V2).
- **G2 fidelity test** (13.8): with the server frozen (`tick freeze` / `tick step`) the test rig was shot from three
  cameras (front and 3/4 at 10 degrees above, side level; 4 screen pixels per model pixel at the target) in three poses,
  and `tools/herogen`'s renderer drew the same poses with its perspective camera (read-only, outputs in a scratch
  folder): a `move.idle` frame, an `attack.1` frame over `combat.idle` (the arm's logged angles fix its clip time,
  0.438 s), and the idle with the head pitched 30 degrees down by the head look. **Every silhouette matches within one
  pixel** in all nine views (`fidelity_game_vs_preview.png`); the preview's outline is about half a pixel fuller (110-145
  edge pixels against 7-54 the other way: the two rasterisers' edge rules). The head-look test also confirms its sign
  (GeckoLib hands the model data the negated pitch, so a positive pitch looks down, as in the preview at +30).
- **Found and fixed (Java):** `v.fc_dhip` was never set (G4's form-scaled kneels, sits, sleep and death played the
  middle form; `HeroModel.applyMolangQueries` now sets it from `leg_r`, pure rule `logic/hero/FormScale`);
  `getDeathMaxRotation` was GeckoLib's 90 degrees (now 0: the clip lays the body down); the face planes took GeckoLib's
  flat-cube normal fix, so a face looking down showed a bright band across the eyes (G2's finding; `HeroRenderer`
  now draws the `eyes`, `mouth` and `cheeks` cubes with their true normals); left-hand items were rendered with the
  right hand's mirror (GeckoLib always passes `leftHand = false`: a shield lay flat on the ground, a torch pointed
  the wrong way; `HeroItemLayer` passes the hand, and A2's shield offset is gone); a teleport counted as running for a
  tick (the base clip flashed to the run and restarted the idle; a step over 3 blocks a tick, `HeroClips.TELEPORT_STEP`,
  is now left out of the ground speed). The render statistics line also gives the frames per second.
- **Render cost (software GL: Mesa llvmpipe on 4 CPU cores, load average 6-9 from the other workflows; not a GPU
  measurement):** a grid of test rigs 3-21 blocks from the camera, full detail. Frames per second (F3): no hero 10, 1
  hero 7-9, 16 heroes 5-7, 64 heroes 4-6 (100, 110-140, 150-200, 170-250 ms a frame). `heroRenderStats` (CPU time in
  the renderer per hero): 5.0 ms with one hero, 2.1-2.9 ms with 16, 0.93-1.29 ms with 64. Section 11's 0.1-0.25 ms and
  "20 heroes under 3 ms" are for a real GPU and stay to be measured on one (V2).
- **For the art pipeline (no Java change):** the renderer still applies the flat-cube fix to the face planes, so its
  previews of a face turned down or to its right are lit unlike the game now (light only; silhouettes match); with
  vanilla-size tools on the adult test rig the pickaxe and axe read well, on the chibi a shovel is nearly as long as
  the body (A2's open item); the test rig's hair loses much of its volume beyond 20 blocks (A2's open item).

Review of A0-A2 and checkpoint 1 (2026-11-12, an adversarial review of everything since `67317d8`, GeckoLib's classes
read in the decompiled 4.9.3 jar):
- **Checked, no change needed.** Dedicated-server safety: nothing in `hero/`, `campaign/` or `command/` reaches a
  client class; `HeroAnimator` uses GeckoLib's common animation classes, `Level.playLocalSound` and the common
  registries only, and GeckoLib builds a hero's controllers only when its manager is first asked for (the renderer).
  Synced data: the hero's six ids follow `FactionNpc`'s four in the class chain, and GeckoLib defines none (its mixins
  add no entity data). Face frames: GeckoLib bakes UVs as texel / the geo file's `texture_width` and `texture_height`
  (`GeoQuad.build` with `ModelProperties`), which `HeroRenderer` divides the shift by, so a 256-high texture works as a
  128-high one does. Speed: `AnimationController.adjustTick` multiplies the speed into the tick it compares with the
  transition length, so 4.8's "GeckoLib scales the whole controller clock, the blend included" holds; the checkpoint's
  +2 ticks at 2x is the viewer's 5-12 frames a second, not an unscaled blend (that open item is closed). `setHidden`
  also hides a bone's children; `saveInitialSnapshot` runs once per bone, so the shared bones' rest poses stay right
  when heroes with different models alternate. Old worlds: a format 2 campaign loads with no heroes and a roster record
  without `hero`; nothing reads the format as a gate. The director rule (counted in the garrison and the war council,
  never in the soldier sort, never retrained), blink determinism (seeded per UUID; low frame rates show a blink late,
  never drop it), the LOD bands, the admin commands' permission level 2, and A0's hashes, jar-in-jar range and the MIT
  licence copies (byte-identical to the jar's own) are as designed.
- **Found and fixed.** (1) `/fc hero info`, open to every player, printed an enemy hero's position, health and entity
  UUID and its home base's coordinates, intelligence players otherwise get only from their scouts; now only operators
  and the hero's own faction see where it and its home are. (2) A hero saved during its death animation (a stop, or its
  chunk unloading, within the second after it fell) loaded as a dying copy that the uniqueness rule took for a hero
  "presumed lost and back": its record turned alive, the copy vanished a second later without dying again, and the
  hero never returned (`revive` refused it as alive); a dead or dying hero entity is now discarded when it joins.
  (3) Per-frame allocations in the render path: the controllers' `has` built a string key with a stream on every call
  (several per hero per frame), bone visibility built two maps, a list and a sort per hero per frame, model, texture and
  phase-2 texture ids were new strings and `ResourceLocation`s on every call (four or more per hero per frame), and so
  were the glow mask id, the `v.fc_dhip` supplier, the sway chain's list and the base controller's candidate list. Now
  `has` answers from a map per resolved chain, the visibility plan is kept per model, phase, fine and face detail and
  weapon flag and only applied each frame, the ids are cached, one Molang supplier serves every hero, the chain list is
  reused, the base clip is resolved again only when the motion's class (`HeroClips.baseKey`) or the chain changes, and
  the renderer looks the hero's client state up once per render instead of once per bone. (4) `/fc admin hero give`
  from another dimension put the hero at the operator's coordinates in the overworld; there it now goes to the base's
  spawn point, and a position given from another dimension is refused. (5) A preview copy that died (`/kill`, an
  elimination) told its sash faction "was killed". (6) Seen in the viewer while checking these fixes: the campaign
  hero, travelling at its ordinary 0.24-0.26 blocks a tick (dips to 0.15 at corners), flipped between `move.walk` and
  `move.run` about a dozen times in 5 s, each flip restarting the clip with a blend; a run now holds down to 0.20
  (`HeroClips.RUN_TO_WALK`) and a walk/run change shows only after 6 ticks (`PACE_HOLD`, both PROPOSED; `PaceGate`, one
  per hero), and 90 s of patrol showed no flip.
- **Tests.** `heroUniquePerId` also loads a stale copy through the chunk-load path (`ServerLevel.addLegacyChunkEntities`,
  NeoForge's `loadedFromDisk` join event; before, only fresh spawns were tested); `heroDiesAndRevives` loads the dying
  copy and checks the record stays fallen; `heroWeaponAndMoodFollowTheFight` lands a real blow and checks the weapon
  stays out 5 s after it (it passed before with the 5 s rule removed: there was no blow); new unit tests
  `motionsWithOneKeyGetTheSameCandidates` (over every motion), `everyClipIsWrittenForTheControllerThatPlaysIt`
  (each clip of the timing files is a base or action clip as the controller that plays it needs) and
  `walkAndRunDoNotFlipAboutTheThreshold`. In the Xvfb viewer on the A1a world's dedicated server, after the render-path
  changes: the lineup of the test rig and the six fixtures draws as before, the forced walk, the swing (its `hit` marker
  at animation tick 10.06 for the file's 10) and the moods play, the weapon bones follow the drawn flag, the campaign
  hero walks, stops and idles on its own; typed by a non-operator player outside Ember, `/fc hero info test_rig` hides
  where it is, inside Ember it shows it. Renderer time per hero 0.6-0.9 ms with one to seven heroes on software GL
  (5.0 ms with one at the checkpoint, on a busier machine: not a controlled comparison). Sabotage checks,
  each caught by its test: drafting heroes like soldiers (`heroNotDraftedIntoRaidByDefault`), letting a dead copy join
  (`heroDiesAndRevives`), checking only fresh spawns, not chunk loads (`heroUniquePerId` at the new assertion), and
  dropping the 5 s weapon rule (`heroWeaponAndMoodFollowTheFight`).
- **Checkpoint 1 stands:** A0, A1a and A2 are done and reviewed; the test rig renders, blinks and walks in game. Left
  as noted: the hero drawn with `_fallback` geometry when a client lacks its own keeps its own look's bone lists
  (harmless while every hero shares the rig's names and face regions); the live check's open items above.

F1 as built (2026-11-13), where it goes beyond or differs from v1 §26 and §29.1 and the row above:
- **Seven pure classes** in `logic/hero/`: `HeroJson` (line and column of every key and value, six forgiven things
  as notes, 21 refusals in plain words), `HeroCatalogue` (every field table, word list with its synonyms, allowlist,
  role preset, growth step, form, trigger, placeholder list, cap and weight constant; no other class holds a number
  of the format), `HeroDef` (records; every list and map is copied when a record is built, so a loaded hero cannot be
  changed), `HeroReader` (files to definitions, the sense rules, the checks across files), `Problems` (outcome, code,
  file, line, friendly and machine path, report and summary), `HeroBalance` (reference Amounts, per-effect and slot
  caps, weights, floors, the ultimate's gap) and `HeroRules` (the runtime arithmetic). Not in F1: skins, the schema
  and guide writers and `checkHeroes` (F2), and anything that runs in the game.
- **Equal treatment in the reader** (6.15): `protects: players` is read as `allies` and the `target_is` kinds
  `player` and `npc` as the kind `person`, each ADJUSTED with the owner's rule in the message
  (`HeroCatalogue.EQUAL_READINGS`, code `equal_treatment`). They are no longer words of the format, so no loaded
  definition can single out players, and a test fails if a `HeroDef` word names them again. The Python reference
  checker still takes them as written (the cross-check cases stay clear of them).
- **Equal treatment in the arithmetic**: `HeroRules` has no input that says whether an attacker or a target is a
  player (a test checks it): one formula, the same clamps and `heroes.maxHit` against anyone, no multiplier against
  players. The slot caps are applied to the definition when it loads, so they hold against any target. The 2% limit
  on `target_max_health_percent` is the caller's `targetIsBoss`, true for a vanilla boss and for a hero (6.2).
- **No yield, no attack tokens**: nothing in F1 knows either. `behaviour.retreat_below` is carried as the file wrote
  it (0: never), for `EscapeRules` to read (6.13).
- **Revival** (3.4): `HeroRules.reviveDelaySeconds(config, own, riteTier)`, `reviveAt` and `revivalDue`: 600 s for
  every hero, a hero's own `revival` seconds first (an hour at most), the faction's `revival_rite` tier shortening
  either by 20/30/40%, never under 30 s; `at: night` waits for nightfall.
- **War power is a hook**: `HeroRules.heroPower` and `HERO_DEFENDERS` still hold v1 §25.7's numbers behind
  `WAR_POWER_IS_PROVISIONAL`, with a test that says so. Section 8's formula, cap and hurt-hero rule are not in the
  code yet.
- **Beyond v1's text** (each tested): a JSON `null` is a field left out (a NOTE; a required one is then "missing");
  the balance pass reports at the exact field and its line (`skills[0].cooldown`, `skills[0].effects[1].seconds`,
  `empowered.stats[1].percent`) and says when the value it raised was a default; placeholders are read in any case;
  at most 200 problems are listed per hero, then one line with the count and the worst outcome among the rest (what
  stops a hero from loading is always listed); a hero without `gear` gets its style's weapon with nothing reported;
  melee, bow, crossbow and trident basic attacks are physical whatever the hero's element (v1 §9); text is never cut
  through a surrogate pair.
- **Tests**: `HeroMessagesTest` makes every one of the 90 reader and 27 JSON codes; `HeroReaderRobustnessTest`
  damages the three examples and a hero that uses every effect type (6,000 seeded files in the suite; a one-off run
  of 160,000 found nothing) and checks every loaded hero against every range, cap, floor and recipient rule;
  `HeroCatalogueTest` compares the catalogue with `herotool.py`'s tables and v1 appendix C; `HeroCrossCheckTest`
  reads the 38 cases `tools/herotool/crosscheck.py` writes with the reference checker's verdicts. Seven sabotage
  checks, each caught by its test: a player-only word back in the format, nulls not skipped, a floor reported at
  the ability instead of the field, a `damageVsPlayers` method, the 30 s revival floor dropped, the problem limit
  removed, a list that can be changed.
- **Left open**: v1's on-hit lists cap damage, healing, shields and stuns but no other control (a file may freeze or
  taunt on every basic hit; a v1 gap, not changed here); a `thrown` hero with both a kit weapon and a `held_item`
  loads with both and the game side must choose which it holds.

F2 as built (2026-11-13), where it goes beyond or differs from sections 12 and 14.1 and the row above:
- **Four new pure classes** in `logic/hero/`: `HeroReader2` (format 2's parts, read inside `HeroReader` once the
  file's format is known; a format-1 file goes through F1's code unchanged), `EscapeRules` (6.13: the kind, where an
  escaping hero stands, `retreat_below` from the personality and `flee_at`; the runtime `decide` comes with F5/A12),
  `HeroSchemaGen` and `HeroCheckMain`. `HeroDef` gains a `V2` part: moves (class, frames, bands, weight, phases,
  `prefer_when`, followups, the four flags, channel, telegraph, the new forms' fields, strikes, per-shot lists, the
  animation id), the ultimate and its awakened twin, states, the boss block, personality and the end of a fight,
  leader, talents, voice, recruit, relationships, revival, mobility and the generated hash. Every word of the format
  knows the format that added it (`Word.inFormat`): format 1's lists are frozen, so a format-1 file reads exactly as
  before, and v1 synonyms that became words of their own (`charge`, `barrier`, `sweep`, `rain`, `barrage`) mean the
  new word only in format 2.
- **Format 1 maps to format 2** silently (`HeroReader2.mapV1`): each skill is a move (its id from its name, its class
  from its form and heavy with a stun, its wind-up raised to the class minimum, a zone's delay folded into the
  wind-up), the ultimate is a move of class `ultimate`, `empowered` a state, base abilities talents, `retreat_below: 0`
  a last stand; no mobility block (MOBILITY_AND_WORK 8.2). In a format-2 file `skills`, `empowered`,
  `base_abilities` and `stats.size` are read as their format-2 parts with a NOTE; `starter_for`, `factions`,
  `skin_model` and `color` are ignored with a NOTE.
- **Equal treatment, no yield, no tokens** (6.15, 15.6) as listed there, and: a test walks every record component
  and enum constant of `HeroDef` and every word list (none names a player, an NPC, a yield, a spare or a token);
  `{player}` stays in non-fight lines (a greeting names whom it greets). `health_scale: { base, players }` keeps its
  base and ignores the per-player part with 12.10's NOTE; `attack_tokens`, `tokens` and `health_per_player` are read
  and ignored the same way.
- **Levels** (8): `unlock_level` on moves, the ultimate, passives and phases, and the `unlock` line event, are read and
  ignored with a NOTE; every loaded part has unlock level 1.
- **The end of a fight** (6.13): the kind from `when_losing`, else a v1 `retreat_below` the file wrote as 0, else the
  nerve a + h - c (65 or more stands; `sleepy` always stands); `stands_for` as written (`[]` is nowhere) or from
  loyalty (home at 70, beside the leader at 85); a `retreat_below` the file leaves out comes from the role's default
  + 20 (c - 0.5) - 10 (a - 0.5), clamped to 0-60, a half rounding up (kit-gaps 11); `flee_at` is a third of it, a half
  rounding up, within 8-15, at most the last phase's start less 10 and never under 5. The 30 temperaments give
  Appendix A's table and the slice heroes SLICE_PLAN's lines ("escapes under 8%, never from its home"; `/fc hero
  check` and `checkHeroes` print them). NOTEs: traits that flip the temperament's usual kind, `stands_for` on a hero
  that fights to the death, a last phase under 18% for an escaping hero.
- **Personality into behaviour** (5.4): traits stay within 20 of the temperament's (ADJUSTED); when `behaviour` names
  no `aggression`, it comes from the trait (65 or more bold, 35 or less cautious), and no `focus` from the
  temperament where it has a rule (strongest: battle maniac, righteous; heroes first: hot-blooded, proud noble;
  weakest: schemer, trickster), else the role's; `duty: court` is read as `guard` (the game sets it while the hero
  leads).
- **Balance** (6.4, 6.5) on top of F1's caps: the class of 6.4 (a lighter class only for a move with no damage, DoT,
  stun, freeze, knockback or pull; a stun makes it heavy); frames raised to the class minimums (none written: the
  minimums, silently); a zone lands as its wind-up ends, so the wind-up covers its delay and the delay becomes 0
  (kit-gaps 5); the decal by form (`HeroCatalogue.decalOf`, kit-gaps 4), `none` only for light moves and never for an
  unblockable one; slot caps by form (a combo's strikes together within 1.25 x the cap, a rain's third of its impacts
  within the zone cap, every barrage shot on the one target with the per-shot lists cycling, a wave's waves as hits);
  6.5's weights for the new forms and their cooldown floors; a state's `on_hit` shares the on-hit cap with the basic
  attack's. The seven new effects weigh (PROPOSED, 6.6 gives none): `guard_break` s/2, `poise` amount/10, `silence`
  2 per s, `tether` s/2, `barrier` 2 per hit, `spawn_add` its count, `call_guards` 1.5 per guard; at most 3 decoys.
- **Format decisions for kit-gaps.md** (each recorded there): 1, a move follows itself only when `repeatable`, `max`
  1-3 caps the repeats, the string limit of 4 counts the repeats and the validator checks the longest string
  through other moves (6.3); 2, the defaults (weight 5, a reaction's chance 50 and cooldown 10, a transition 1 s,
  energy gain 10, charge rate 100) and a NOTE for an ultimate without its line; 5, a strike carries its own effects,
  and "+60% from behind" is a second damage with a `target_behind` condition; 7, a reaction whose move is not in the
  current phase waits for a phase that has it (no message: Benika and Kagenori answer a shield only from phase 2);
  8, `channel` is a move field (true, a percent or `{ interrupt }`, 15% by default) and a `channel` weak point
  without a channelled move is a NOTE; 12, enrage after 180 s, 240 s as leader; 13, a flyer's phases come from
  `mobility.aerial` (Ambrose: phase 2); the `dive` form stays F5/A12's.
- **Sound references** (SOUND.md 3, 5): format 2 checks only a sound id's form (`minecraft:<event>`, a bare vanilla
  event, `factionclash:hero.cue.*`, `hero.kit.*`, `hero.voice.<id>` or a hero's own set; `HeroCatalogue.SOUND_ID_REGEX`
  is also the schema's pattern); format 1 keeps its allowlist.
- **Mobility** (MOBILITY_AND_WORK 8): 8.2's defaults (the archetype's first allowed primary of `check_roster.py` at
  tier 1, its flight carrier, aerial phases 2-3, or 1-3 for a legendary flyer, `water` avoided by a hero with a water
  weak point) and 8.3's rules, each ADJUSTED or a NOTE: an unknown or footing-only primary, tier 3 only for epic and
  legendary heroes, a secondary of at most min(2, the primary's tier) that pairs with the primary and the element,
  `aerial` only for a flyer, a flyer's carrier, the aerial numbers in range.
- **Across built-in files** (NOTEs): `gender`, `archetype`, `personality` and `leader` present; leader names and short
  names not taken twice; the uniqueness signature (archetype, element, basic attack style, the ultimate's form and the
  signature move's form and effects) not shared;
  relationships that name a loaded hero; flyers at most 15% of 8 or more heroes.
- **`checkHeroes`**: `./gradlew checkHeroes [-PheroDir=<folder>,...] [-PheroStrict]` runs `HeroCheckMain` (in `check`):
  the folders read as built-in heroes, the report, each hero's end-of-fight line and the summary; it fails on NOT
  LOADED or PART LEFT OUT (`--strict`: on anything). The default folder `src/main/resources/data/factionclash/heroes`
  does not exist until C1-C4 write it, so it passes with nothing to check; on the slice fixtures it prints "Heroes: 8
  loaded".
- **Schema**: `HeroSchemaGen` writes `docs/heroes-v2/hero2.schema.json` (draft 2020-12, `./gradlew heroSchema`) from
  the catalogue, and `HeroSchemaGenTest` fails when the file is stale. It describes a clean file: canonical words,
  values within range, `_` comments anywhere, `unlock_level`, `skills`, `empowered`, `base_abilities`, `stats.size`
  and the `unlock` event marked deprecated; rules across fields stay the validator's. The eight slice fixtures
  validate with Python's `jsonschema` (Draft202012Validator) and with the test's subset validator, and broken copies
  (an unknown field, a yield line, a removed boss field, `target_is_player`, a bad sound id, another effect's field,
  format 3) are refused.
- **Tests** (140 new, 613 unit tests in all): `HeroReaderV2Test` (42: every new field, form, effect, condition,
  trigger and line event, the boss block, the end of a fight, leader to mobility, sounds, the generated block, the
  format-1 mapping, the limits, and 2,000 seeded damaged slice kits that must load sane or be refused),
  `HeroBalanceV2Test` (18), `EscapeRulesTest` (10, against Appendix A's table), `HeroCatalogueV2Test` (7, against
  `check_roster.py` and Appendix A), `SliceKitsTest` (7: the fixtures load clean, keep every format-2 invariant
  (`V2Sanity`), end their fights as SLICE_PLAN says, and each number they lowered to a cap is shown to be over it),
  `HeroSchemaGenTest` (3), `HeroCheckMainTest` (4), and one `HeroMessagesTest` case per new message code (50).
- **The slice fixtures** (`src/test/resources/heroes/slice/`, test data, not the authored heroes of C1-C4): where
  SLICE_PLAN's number is over a cap the fixture writes the cap (Natsuho's `nine_lives` shield 4, not 8: the passive
  shield cap; her `frenzy` bleed 0.5 per second for 2 s: the basic attack's bleed already uses most of the shared
  on-hit cap) and a cooldown SLICE_PLAN leaves open is at or above its floor (`three_cut` 10, not 12.18's 6).
- **Left open**: the `dive` form and `EscapeRules.decide` (F5/A12); the boss bar's title (`boss.intro.title`, else the
  hero's `title`, else its name alone: a presentation choice for A3); the schema does not encode each form's own
  fields (the reader's NOTE); war power is still F1's hook; the guide writer (`HeroDocs`) is not written.

F3 as built (2026-11-13), where it goes beyond or differs from section 6, `MOBILITY_AND_WORK.md` 2.3-2.4, 5.1 and 7.7
and the row above (all pure, none read by the game yet; A3-A6 and A12 wire them in):
- **`BossRules`**: boss health (`healthScale(authored, leading, boss.healthScale)`, `maxHealth(baseMax, scale,
  roleFactor)`, the modifier's value, the share kept when the maximum changes), with no attacker or player count; the
  per-hit cap for any attacker with its excess to poise (`capHit`, `land`: damage with no attacker is neither capped
  nor poise); the hurt cooldown per attacker (`hurtCooldown`, and `HurtLedger` keyed by the attacking entity with one
  shared key for no attacker, dropping records after 10 ticks; `invulnerableTime` and `lastHurt` for 15.6's
  `HeroEntity.hurt`); poise by weapon kind, the parry (shield up 5-11 ticks, a heavy move or heavier), `Poise` with
  decay, the 2.5 s stagger, the maximum x1.2 per stagger and x1.25 enraged; the damage-taken windows (stagger +25,
  recovery +15, from behind in the recovery +15 more, downed +20, iron or gold +15 on that weakness, the `back` and
  `recovery` weak points doubling theirs, super armour halving the sum) and the other weak points' tests; the fight
  clock (`engaged`, `Fight`: 15 s memory, 32 blocks, the end 10 s after the last enemy, healing 30 s after at 1% of
  the base maximum a second); enrage (180 s, 240 s leading, 120-300 authored, a hard trigger at once) and the +25%
  ceiling on the boss layer's bonuses (maluses count on top); phases forward only, the re-arm 10% above, the
  transition's 0.8-2.0 s, the rebirth and its pip; the intro once a day against people; hazard-aware knockback; the
  receiver's stun immunity (`stunImmuneUntil`: at most 2 s stopped in any 6 s, however many heroes try); `AirPoise`
  (`MOBILITY_AND_WORK.md` 2.4); the fight's ceiling from the highest engaged enemy and `ReachClock` (4 s out of
  reach, held by a telegraphed wind-up, off while escaping).
- **`MoveSelector`** with **`Sense`** (what the hero senses at one check, 15.6's `TargetState` inside it, with no
  field that says who anyone is) and **`Conditions`** (every condition of both formats and v1's `use_when`): the gap
  by phase and enrage, the opening gap (a full phase-1 gap from the fight's start or the intro's end, never
  shortened: nothing lands before 1.9 s, 2.3 s heavy, 3.0 s ultimate, tested over 200 seeds), v1's 1 s between two
  starts, the score of 6.3, charges and cooldowns with v1's refunds (a cancelled start costs nothing, an interrupted
  wind-up gets half its cooldown back), the ultimates (full energy and their least gap), followup strings, reactions,
  the arena and footing filters (water, a hazard on the dash path, a low ceiling, airborne, clinging), the
  chuunibyou's opener, and `frames(move, speed)` that never shortens a wind-up or a recovery below its class
  minimum. No token check anywhere; nothing is picked while the hero is stunned, silenced or escaping.
- **Decisions** (recorded in `notes/kit-gaps.md`, "F3 decisions"): a reaction rolls once per occurrence of its
  condition (again only when its cooldown has passed while the condition holds), and its answer waits for the gap; a
  followup needs its own charge and reach, not the gap, and a move repeating itself is the same use (one charge for
  zoomies' four bounds); postures by form (self, ally, allies and counter defensive; zone, wave and rain zoning; the
  rest offensive); the `phase` condition is the current phase exactly; the back bonus is part of the punish window
  (kit-gaps 8); super armour holds poise just short of its maximum so the next hit staggers; the ground decals of
  every form (kit-gaps 4).
- **`Decals`**: flat boxes for block displays: a disc is two squares of side 2r cos 22.5 degrees, so the star they
  draw never falls inside its circle (its points reach about 31% beyond); strips along the aim; fans of 3-5 ribs (one
  per 30 degrees); rings of 6-12 strips tangent outside the radius; the display translation for a piece spawned at its
  centre and the scale-0 translation that keeps its grow point fixed (strips grow from the hero, discs from their
  centre); the colours (red ultimate, purple unblockable, else the accent's dye); `growTicks`; the budget of 12 pieces
  (a disc falls back to one square of side 2r, a fan or ring thins, the rest waits).
- **`EscapeRules`, the runtime half** (6.13, `MOBILITY_AND_WORK.md` 7.7): `lowHealth` (strictly under `flee_at`:
  escape, or the last-stand beat once), `lostRaid`, `lostBase` (the home stand ends with the heart), `heldBy` (home
  while the heart stands, beside the fighting leader), `mayEscape` (the window of `heroes.escapeCooldownSeconds`,
  `heroes.escape`; an unspent `rebirth` holds only the health trigger), `cornered` (3 s), `escapeOver` (the refuge's
  walls or 10 s clear of pursuers within 48), `mayReturn` (`heroes.escapeAwaySeconds` and above half), `refuge`
  (never the base it flees: home, else the nearest standing base, else the wild) and `heroPursues` (honour 60 lets it
  go). `EscapeRulesTest` walks the sequences of 7.7 ("Why it cannot loop").
- **Rewards** (`HeroRules`): `rewardXp` (7 a level, at most 100), `winningSide` (the side of the last person who hurt it
  in its last 15 s; a member player and its faction's NPCs are one side, a factionless player a side of one) and
  `crestKey` (hero, side, in-game day).
- **`HeroActions`**: bit 7 of `DATA_STATE` is `STATE_ESCAPING` (15.6).
- **Tests** (61 new, 674 unit tests in all): `BossRulesTest` (23, with `referenceDuel`, which gives SLICE_PLAN
  criterion 15's table to the second, and `equalForPlayerAndNpc`, which walks every method, parameter, field and record
  component of the new classes for a name that singles out players, NPCs, tokens or a yield), `MoveSelectorTest` (18),
  `ConditionsTest` (4), `DecalsTest` (7), `EscapeRulesTest` (+8), `HeroRulesTest` (+1).
- **Left open**: the `dive` form and the air budget (F5); decals' block states and the access transformer (A3);
  everything that reads these classes in the game.

F4 as built (2026-11-13), where it goes beyond or differs from sections 5, 7.2, 8.1 and the row above (pure, in
`logic/leader/` but for the war power; L1-L5 wire them in):
- **`Personality`**: `t_eff = clamp(0.5 + k (t - 0.5), 0, 1)`, `k` from `leaders.personalityStrength` (0-200%), the
  20-point rule (`adjust`, the reader's rule as a function), the fight AI's raw readings (the aggression word,
  `retreat_below`), the noise (sigma 0.2 x v, doubled for an airhead), and 8's compatibility, bond term and loyalty.
- **`Temperaments`**: Appendix A's rows (traits from `HeroCatalogue`, the special rule, economy focus, recruit
  condition, admires and clashes; tested row by row against the appendix), the affinity (+0.2 when either admires the
  other, -0.2 when either clashes, 0 for both or neither) and the flags of the rules milestone 1 acts on (territorial,
  builds ahead, opener, doubled noise, doubled ambush, always spares, no raids before day 3, vengeance at any
  aggression, bold under half health, strongest beatable); the others wait for their systems.
- **`LeaderBiases`**: every knob of 5.5 (raids, odds, garrison, defenders, fall-back, the leader's raids, soldier cap,
  swordsmen, ranged share, trader, enchanter, recruitment, food reserve, house, storage, repair budget, trader trips,
  keep-back, gold and diamonds wanted, need order, the raid target cost with `fd`, `fdist`, loot, lairs and the
  ambush, the mercy rule, the vengeance raid, the fall-back on a leader's loss, the noise). The economy focus and
  the temperament rules are off at `k = 0`. A half rounds up (also below zero: -0.5 is 0), which gives the values
  the kit sheets printed where the formula lands on a half (Hinae's trader at 11.5 is 12, Kagenori's 17.5 is 18,
  Ambrose's 13.5 is 14, his repair budget 395.5 is 396). `LeaderBiasesTest` reads `FCConfig`, `FactionDirector` and
  `TraderJob` and fails when one of today's constants changes without the neutral value.
- **`LeaderDraft`**: the seeded greedy draw (weights 4, 4, 2, 1; 200 tries a round) with the rules relaxed from the
  last to the first (5, 4, 3, then rule 2's delta-E, then rule 1), distinct dyes never relaxed (a slot no hero can
  fill under it stays empty, for the legacy identity), fixed leaders first, the seed (`leaders.seed`, else world seed
  XOR the start time).
- **`FactionNameGen`**: the leader's first unused name and its short name (else the name's key word, its last two
  words or its last word), else a seeded name from colour words by the primary dye, three motif words for each of
  the 54 archetypes and group nouns by temperament ("Crimson Anvil Warband", "Oath of the Indigo Crane", "The Ashen
  Watch"); names, short names and slugs unique in the campaign (slot ids included), slugs `[a-z0-9_]` 3-24;
  base names ("Still Crane Keep").
- **`Palette`**: Lab (D65), CIE76 delta-E, the nearest dye, chat and boss bar colour, the identity dyes of 5.1 (the
  banner's dyes when there is a banner) and hex text. The test keeps 5.1's examples: indigo and charcoal land on
  gray, plum on black. Indigo's nearest chat colour is dark gray, not dark blue (only the scoreboard glow uses it).
- **`BannerDesign`**: the leader's banner when it is valid (2-4 vanilla layers, never the logo), else the archetype's
  emblem (Appendix C's ten groups, each of the 54 archetypes assigned to one; the sea group adds a border as its
  second layer) in the faction's dyes, a layer in the base's own dye turned white or black, whichever reads; the
  `/give` component text.
- **`BaseStyles`**: Appendix B's 16 families (blocks, stairs, seat, milestone; tested against the table), the
  `STONE_BLOCKS` additions, the archetype's family (ROSTER_PLAN 2.3, tested against its table), and for a family not
  built yet the nearest built one by the colours of its walls, roofs and perimeter, walls counting double (coral port
  waits as tide port, ivory palace as frost hall, jungle haven as grove lodge, tuff workshop as deepslate keep, mud
  town as grove lodge, desert court as tide port); a default culture by ROSTER_PLAN's archetype groups (east for
  A-C, west for D, mixed for E).
- **`Talents`**: the 35 talents' tier numbers (tested against 7.2's table), each working at tier I, tiers by level,
  the milestones (15 live in milestone 1), the strongest copy at a base and the realm rule, `mentor`'s recruit levels;
  kit-gaps 9's combinations: `sage_of_letters` and the leader's enchanter level take the lower, `warden_of_walls`
  multiplies the leader's repair budget.
- **War power** (`HeroRules`, 8.1): `heroSoldiers`, `heroPower(maxHealth, level, war.heroPowerPercent)`,
  `countedPower` (the soldier backbone) and `defencePower` (half under half health) replace the hook;
  `WAR_POWER_IS_PROVISIONAL` and `HERO_DEFENDERS` are gone. The test holds 8.1's five rows, the worked raid (25.3
  against 22.4, 0.60 with Benika dead) and the cap; `BossRulesTest.referenceDuel` prints each slice hero's power
  (Benika leading: 5.3 iron soldiers, 11.8).
- **The kit sheets** (`docs/heroes-v2/kits/*.txt`, "How ... personality steers the faction"): all eight agree with
  `LeaderBiases` number for number (`LeaderBiasesTest.theSliceKitSheetsAgreeNumberForNumber`).
- **Tests** (43 new, 717 unit tests in all): `PersonalityTest` (5), `TemperamentsTest` (3), `LeaderBiasesTest` (9),
  `LeaderDraftTest` (5), `FactionNameGenTest` (4), `PaletteTest` (5), `BannerDesignTest` (3), `BaseStylesTest` (4),
  `TalentsTest` (4) and `HeroRulesTest`'s two war power tests in place of the hook's. Seven sabotage checks, each
  caught.
- **Left open**: the game hooks (L1-L5); the special rules that need milestone 2 systems; the boss bar colours are
  approximations of the bar textures.

F5 as built (2026-11-13), where it goes beyond or differs from `MOBILITY_AND_WORK.md` sections 1-3, 5, 7, 8 and 9-13
and the row above (all pure, in `logic/hero/`, none read by the game yet; A10-A12 and L5m wire them in):
- **`MobilityRules`**: the nine families and two footings with their full numbers by ceiling, the features a ceiling
  gives (all from level 1; counts such as air jumps are features), the power `p(L)` with both config keys, the three
  kinds of number, link sizes rounded down and the floors (a leaper's 1.8-block jump, a 2-block gap); a hero's
  `Profile` at its level (primary and secondary together, the better number counting; `NONE` for workers, format-1
  heroes and `mobility.enabled` off; `mobility.flight` off keeps the glide and the dive), the vanilla attribute
  modifiers named `factionclash:mobility_<trait>` (travel bonuses only while travelling), flight stamina with its drain,
  regain, the 25% landing and the exhaustion until 50%, the average flight speed of 2.1, the take-off refusals, the
  stamina reserve, the air budget (8 s a take-off, 6 s on the ground, 4 in phase 3, 40% of any 30 s; a raid's
  approach 10 s), the ceilings of 2.3, which rule of 5.1 holds while fighting, travelling, on a raid's approach and
  while escaping, the reach clock's input from engaged enemies of any kind, the bursts, and the sheet of levels 1, 10
  and 20 (`checkHeroes` prints it). Its tests hold 15.9's table for the eight slice heroes.
- **`LeapSolver`**: vanilla's air physics (vertical (v - 0.08) x 0.98, horizontal x 0.91, the vertical move resolved
  before the horizontal) on a vertical slice with fences 1.5 high; jump heights 1.25 / 1.84 / 2.52 / 3.54 for 0.42 /
  0.52 / 0.62 / 0.75 and the attribute a height needs; the lowest-speed impulse on a 0.01 grid that lands within
  0.15 of the cell centre with the hero's box clear along the arc (1.6's four leaps come out exactly), the table of
  3.9 with a solve within the hero's limits, the in-air re-check and an acrobat's air-jump correction, and the
  take-off refusals (water, powder snow, a cobweb, honey, soul sand, Slowness, Levitation, Slow Falling, moving).
- **`FlightPlanner`**: stations every 4 blocks with 2-block side samples, one 24-block waypoint at a time and only
  over loaded terrain, the cruise layer 6-10 blocks over the segments it ends and starts, the ceilings, the
  hostile-base berth (x3 within the walls box + 16 on a trip or an escape, a cost and never a ban; the line on a raid),
  legal landings, the take-off headroom over the footprint, the forward probe, the stuck watch (10 ticks of head bumps
  or under 1 block in 2 s) and the walk-or-fly choice of 3.5.
- **`HeroLinks`**: jump-up, gap (from the leap solver), drop, climb (round a one-block overhang at III), chimney and
  glide links on a pure grid, at most 8 a node and only where a worker's own step fails; hazards refused, risky gaps
  refused in a fight and allowed with a wide landing out of one or while escaping; a hostile wall is climbed like any
  wall; who can reach the hero (an ordinary person, a player or an NPC soldier alike, and an enemy hero on its own
  links), and the strike from a link (a basic attack only, only with no standable cell in reach).
- **`WallEntry`** (new): the way in by the hero's whole mobility, taken only with a way out (the gate, flight with
  its stamina back within 20 s, the inside face climbed or jumped), the soldiers' way otherwise (a charger leads the
  breach when the gate does not open for them), the goal inside (open the way, hunt the hero, clear the archers, the
  storehouse, the heart) and its end (its own condition, the raid's end or 60 s without progress), the campaign
  check's counter.
- **`EscapeRules`**, the rest of 7.7: `decide` dispatches the three triggers; a scout's step aside and retreat use an
  escape's movement and nothing else (`Departure`); who pursues; the wild's 64 blocks; no rewards for an escape; the
  morale changes; the war council while away.
- **`WorkMastery`** (new): today's worker numbers (read from the sources by the test), proficiency by tier at the
  duty, the specialties of every production talent with the roster's duties (`TALENT_DUTY`, read from
  `check_roster.py`), `aura` stacks over the cap, virtual enchantment levels, extra rolls, the one-or-the-other rule,
  furnace progress, prices, repairs, trips, `sage_of_letters`' rerolls, Presence's tier IV and the personal effects,
  every cap of 13 and the output flag (the worked 2.75x day).
- **The `dive` form** (7.3, 8.3): format 2's ninth new form (`range` 4-12, `radius` 1-3, `from` air or apex), heavy,
  a landing decal, weight as a leap + 1, at least 1.0 s of recovery; refused (`dive_rule`, PART LEFT OUT) for a hero
  without an airborne trait, `from: air` without flight or gliding, an unblockable dive and a dive that leads into a
  grab; the move selector starts an air dive only in the air and an apex dive with its jump. The schema is
  regenerated. Decisions: `docs/heroes-v2/notes/kit-gaps.md`, "F5 decisions".
- **Tests** (76 new, 793 unit tests in all): `MobilityRulesTest` (17), `LeapSolverTest` (7), `FlightPlannerTest` (10),
  `HeroLinksTest` (13), `WallEntryTest` (9), `WorkMasteryTest` (11), `EscapeRulesTest` (+4), `DiveFormTest` (4) and
  one `HeroMessagesTest` case. Ten sabotage checks, each caught.
- **Left open**: everything that reads these classes in the game (A10-A12, L5m); the touch-and-go string of dives
  (0.5 s touch-downs, `HeroCatalogue.DIVE_TOUCH_DOWN`) is the selector's in A12; the slice fixtures keep Ambrose's
  `broom_hops` (his `broom_dive` is C3's proposal); hover, deep swimming and lava walking are milestone 2's (A13), and
  their numbers are in the tables but not exercised by a fixture.

A1b as built (2026-11-13), where it goes beyond or differs from 3.2-3.4, 12.1, 12.15, 8.1 and the row above:
- **The definitions.** `hero/HeroDefinitions` reads every `data/factionclash/heroes/*.json` of the server's packs when
  the server loads its data (`/reload` too) and hands them to `HeroRegistry`. The design names
  `SimpleJsonResourceReloadListener`; this is its sibling `SimplePreparableReloadListener` on the same folder (the same
  `FileToIdConverter`, so a datapack replaces a file the same way) reading the text, because Gson's tree has lost the
  lines every problem names and refuses a file with a comment before the checker could say why. Only the mod's own
  heroes load (the owner's answer of 2026-11-12): a definition whose id the mod does not ship is NOT LOADED
  (`not_builtin`), files of another namespace are skipped. Then `config/factionclash/heroes/<id>.json` overrides are
  merged (`logic/hero/HeroOverride`: each top-level field replaces the built-in's whole field, `id`, `art`,
  `animations` and `format` stay the built-in's with a NOTE, an unreadable override is PART LEFT OUT and the built-in
  loads as it is), and every problem in a field the override wrote names the override file and its own line
  (`HeroReader.Source` carries the merged tree and which file each member came from). `logic/hero/HeroAssembly` puts it
  together with a test's fixtures and checks all of it as built-in heroes; `config/factionclash/heroes/_report.txt` is
  rewritten after every load. `/fc hero reload` reads the packs, the overrides and the visual files again, applies the
  result to every loaded hero at once and prints the summary with every NOT LOADED and PART LEFT OUT line; `/reload`
  does the same through the listener. `/fc hero check <id|all>` reads and checks them now, applies nothing, and prints
  the problems and the hero's sheet (role, boss health at levels 1, its own and 30 with its war power, kit, moves, the
  end of its fights, revival, talents, lines).
- **Entries.** A hero is listed when it has a visual file or a loaded definition; `HeroEntry.def` is its checked
  definition, null for a dormant one. A definition with no art of its own is drawn with `_fallback` and its fists style;
  preview fixtures need none.
- **Dormant** (3.4): no valid definition (removed or broken; an override that would break it is left out instead, so
  the built-in hero loads: "Review of F1-F5 and A1b" below). The entity keeps its UUID,
  faction, saved state, last role (`HeroState.role`, also for a dormant return) and kit; it has a plain soldier's
  numbers (20 health, nothing added, the share kept), the neutral model and size, `(dormant)` on its tag, the generic
  lines only, and it cannot be given. A synced `DATA_DORMANT` flag tells clients (a client of a dedicated server knows no
  definitions) which model to draw. A good definition brings it back at once. v1's "last known good" copy is not kept,
  as 3.4 says.
- **Numbers** (`logic/hero/HeroLoadout`): `MAX_HEALTH` base = the v1 health with growth, the one boss modifier
  `factionclash:boss_health`, and `hero_attack`, `hero_armor`, `hero_speed`, `hero_knockback` modifiers, re-applied at a
  spawn, a load, a level-up and a reload with the share of health kept; vanilla reads a saved hero's health before its
  maximum is back, so the saved value is read first and set again. Nothing reads who engages the hero. Leading is
  false until L4 (+50); `boss.healthScale` (25-300%) is a new config key, `[boss]`.
- **Role and kit.** The role from the basic attack, the main-hand item and the style (`HeroLoadout.roleId`: a mace is an
  axe fighter's weapon, as Benika's club). The kit is the file's `gear` as bound vanilla items with names and
  enchantments. The fists rule: the reader now marks the stone sword it adds to a melee kit with no weapon
  (`GearItem.added`), and a fists-style hero leaves it out and fights bare-handed (an authored weapon such as Natsuho's
  iron "Claw Gauntlets" stays). Three hooks on `FactionNpc` keep a hero's weapon its own: `needsRoleTool` (no trip to
  the storehouse while it holds its kit weapon or fights bare-handed), `takesStoreWeapons` (only a better weapon of its
  role's kind; a mace or bare hands, none) and `takesShield` (only a kit with a shield). Thrown heroes fight
  bare-handed until A3's `HeroThrowGoal`. A reload re-equips a living hero only when its kit changed.
- **Name tag and energy.** `★ Name · title` in the hero's colour (or its faction's), `[Fighting N%]` while it has a
  target (`heroes.showEnergy`), `(dormant)`, `(preview)`. Energy is v1 14's minimal part for the tag: landed melee
  basic hits, kills, damage taken (ranged hits and the ultimate come with A3); saved, 0 after a revival.
- **Lines** (`logic/hero/HeroSpeech`): its own line or a short generic one (`heroes.defaultLines`; each uses only its
  event's placeholders), v1's rate limit (`Clock`), the audiences (nearby within `heroes.dialogueRadius`, the
  faction, the one player greeted; `heroes.dialogue` `local`, `faction`, `off`), chat as `<★ Name> line` and a vanilla
  text display 0.75 above the head for 3 s while the face's talking flag is on; a bubble saved by a stopping server is
  dropped when its chunk loads. Wired now: `summoned`, `greeting` (a faction player's right click), `idle`,
  `fight_start`, `kill`, `low_health`, `victory`, `ally_fallen`, `level_up`, `fallen` (with `{enemy}`) and `revived`
  (the revival block's own line first). The others come with their systems (moves, phases, enrage, escape and last
  stand: A3-A6, A12; founding and leader news: L4).
- **Revival.** `HeroManager.reviveSeconds`: the hero's own `revival.seconds` or `heroes.reviveSeconds`, shortened by
  the faction's best `revival_rite` tier (any hero it owns, alive or fallen, the fallen one included, at the tier of
  its own level: 7.2's "strongest copy counts"), never under `heroes.minReviveSeconds` (30, config range 30-3600, so it
  can only be raised). `where_it_fell` rises at `HeroState.fellAt`, `nearest_base` at the faction's standing base
  nearest to it, `at: night` waits for nightfall. Totem particles; the killer's faction hears of the fall.
- **War council** (8.1, ahead of L3): `soldierPower` over ordinary soldiers; `defencePower` = soldiers at home x
  `soldierPower` + the home heroes' war power (alive, at home, not raiding; half under half health; from the loaded
  entity or the record's last health and maximum), with the backbone cap; the target score in soldier units; the
  party's power through `countedPower` (no hero goes on a raid until L4); `war.heroPowerPercent` (100; 0 counts a hero as
  one head, as before). `FactionDirector.LAST_COUNCIL` keeps each faction's last council with both sides' power and
  whether the heroes decided it. With no hero anywhere every decision is today's (`LeaderBiasesTest` still finds the
  same expressions in the source).
- **`sim.py`** (SLICE_PLAN 6.4): a `council` line per faction and a `hero` line per hero: owner, alive or returns in,
  level, war power, fights by who the enemy was (player, soldiers, hero, monsters), kills, deaths, revivals, casts per
  move, phase reached, staggers, escapes, last stands (the last five stay 0 until A3-A6 count them), health, energy,
  role, what it holds, status, `DORMANT`. `/fc admin dump` has a `heroes` array and each faction's `council`.
- **The test rig's definition**, `data/factionclash/heroes/test_rig.json`, is written by hand (herogen's kit step does
  not write server files yet): a common striker with an iron "Test Blade", stoic (it fights to the death), never a
  leader, a few lines. `checkHeroes` passes with nothing to report. Test fixtures registered with a visual file only
  get a minimal format-2 definition for their style (`HeroRegistry.fixtureDefinition`).
- **Tests**: `HeroOverrideTest` (9), `HeroAssemblyTest` (7), `HeroLoadoutTest` (8), `HeroSpeechTest` (6), two in
  `HeroRulesTest` and four message cases (829 unit tests in all); the GameTests `heroTakesItsDefinition`,
  `fistsHeroHoldsNothingAndFightsBareHanded`, `heroHealthIsTheSameForAPlayerOrAnNpc`, `heroIsDormantWithoutAValidDefinition`,
  `reloadAppliesAChangedDefinitionAtOnce`, `heroCheckReportsABrokenFile`, `revivalBlockShortensTheReturn`,
  `revivalRiteShortensWithTheThirtySecondFloor`, `warCouncilCountsHeroPower` and `savedSpeechBubbleIsDropped` (159 in
  all). Sixteen sabotage checks (eleven in the game, five in the pure rules), each caught; one (a mace's role) was
  missed at first and caught after a test was added for it.
- **Left open**: the moves themselves (A3/A5; the sheet lists them), the thrown basic attack (A3), the boss mechanics
  (A6), the duty's job (heroes still work as guards of their combat role until L5m), leading and raids (L4), ranged
  hits' energy, the station screen's hero sheet, and a `council` line from a campaign long enough for a war council to
  find a target.
- **Checked on a dedicated server** (a fresh world, two in-game days; HANDOFF section 5): the definitions load from
  the jar, `check`, `reload` with an override, a datapack's broken `test_rig.json` making the hero dormant on `/reload`
  and its removal bringing it back, its fall and return, its fights and lines over two days, and a restart. Found there
  and fixed: `/reload` kept the overrides of the last load; it now reads the config folder again.

Review of F1-F5 and A1b (2026-11-13), an adversarial pass over the format code, the pure rules and the game side
against HERO_FORMAT, sections 3.5, 6 (as rewritten for equal treatment, no attack tokens and no yield), 12 and 14.1,
`MOBILITY_AND_WORK.md`, SLICE_PLAN section 3 and the kit sheets:
- **Swept and clean.** No Minecraft class in `logic/`; no player branch in a rule (the `Player` and `ServerPlayer`
  checks left in `hero/` are presentation or interface: who hears a line, the greeting on a right click, the `sim.py`
  count of who a fight was against, and the existing hostility test of `CombatRules`); no attack token anywhere (the
  word survives only as v1's hero tokens and the removed boss fields' NOTEs); nothing yields (`STATE_ESCAPING`, no
  yield goal, line or trigger; the removed words are read and refused as 12.10 and 12.11 say). Every number of 6.2-6.13
  the rules hold was checked against the code (role factors and poise, health scales, the 12% cap, the gaps, bands,
  novelty and posture, class minimums, the new forms' ranges and weights, poise factors, stagger, enrage, phases,
  `flee_at`, the nerve rule, the 10-minute revival with its 30 s floor, war power and the backbone): they agree. The
  eight slice fixtures were compared with SLICE_PLAN 3 move by move (form, class, band, frames, phases, effects,
  reactions, traits, boss block, passives, leader, talents, duty, lines).
- **Found and fixed:**
  - **Heroes withdrew like soldiers.** `HeroEntity` inherited `FactionNpc`'s `WithdrawGoal`, so a hero under 30% of its
    boss health with a target stepped back to eat and came back at 60%, against 3.5, 6.1 and the owner's rule (escape
    or fight to the death). `FactionNpc.withdrawsToRecover` (read once while the goals register) is false for a hero;
    GameTest `heroNeverWithdrawsToEatAsSoldiersDo` (a soldier beside it in the same state withdraws).
  - **An override could take a hero away.** An override the reader could not parse left the built-in hero as it was,
    but one that parsed and broke the hero (a wrong type in a required field) made it dormant. Now `HeroAssembly` does
    not apply an override that leaves its hero unable to load: the built-in hero loads as it is, the report says so
    (`override_breaks`, PART LEFT OUT, in the override's file) and lists the reasons at the override's own lines, and
    the hero is not recorded as overridden (v1 26.5 and 25.5: a file never takes a hero away).
  - **`boss.poise: 0` would stagger on every hit.** 12.10 allows 0 (a hero that never staggers, with another weakness)
    and the reader keeps it, but `BossRules.Poise` raised a base of 0 to 1, so the first point of poise damage would
    have staggered it once A6 wires it. A base of 0 is now no bar at all (`Poise.none`).
  - **Messages for long word lists named the wrong document.** An unknown archetype, talent, format-2 condition or line
    event, or banner pattern said "Allowed: the list in HERO_FORMAT.md Appendix B", which lists none of them; each list
    now names its own (ROSTER_PLAN 2.3, 7.2, HERO_FORMAT 11 with 6.6 and `MOBILITY_AND_WORK.md` 7.5, HERO_FORMAT 23
    with 12.15, 12.12).
  - **A reordered file's phase NOTE** named the phase by its fight order under a path that numbers it by its place in
    the file ("phase 2: phase 3 only changes numbers"); it now says "phase 3 of the fight (from 30%)".
  - **Two kit details the fixtures had lost**: Yugen's `contract` marks only an attacker in melee reach (SLICE_PLAN 3.2's
    "by melee": `if target_close` 4), and Ambrose's `great_brew` rains for 2 s after its 3 s channel (its `duration`
    was 3).
  - **A dive was not a gap closer** for 6.12's NOTE (a hero whose only way to an enemy on a pillar was a dive got "no
    gap closer"); it lands on its target as a leap does, and now counts (`HeroCatalogue.gapCloser`).
- **Robustness.** Every place of every slice kit and v1 example was given each of 16 wrong values (every JSON type,
  empty, out of range) and read alone and as an operator's override: 57,232 reads, no exception, and every hero that
  loaded passed `V2Sanity`. `HeroReaderV2Test.everyPlaceOfEverySliceKitTakesJunkWithoutBreakingARule` keeps a fast
  slice of it (each place one wrong value, about 3,000 reads, the override path asserting the hero always loads).
- **Sabotage.** Twelve deliberate breaks, each caught: `target_is: player` read as a player kind, `flee_at` above the
  last phase, the cap's excess not reaching poise, a 10 s revival floor, boss health without the role factor, a
  string of 5, a light stunning move; and of this review's fixes, a zero poise that staggers, a broken override
  applied, the long lists naming Appendix B, the fixture's contract firing on arrows, a dive that is no gap closer,
  and the withdrawal back on a hero (the GameTest failed with "the hero withdrew like a soldier").
- **Left open** (each a later task's): a leader's third phase (6.7: "epic, legendary and every leader have 3"; a rare or
  common leader still reads its file's two, L4 decides how the third is made); Ilsabet's `rime_wall` shields allies
  within 6 but taunts within 5 (one move has one radius; the fixture uses 5 for both, C4); the great brew's "one of
  three effects at random" is three 33% chances in the fixture (the format has no pick-one list, C3). 836 unit tests,
  160 GameTests.

A3 as built (2026-11-13), where it goes beyond or differs from 3.3, 3.5, 6.3, 6.14-6.15, HERO_FORMAT 9-16 and 32
phase 4, and the row above:
- **The loop.** `hero/HeroCombat` is a hero's fight runtime (made on first use): its `MoveSelector` (built from the
  definition, seeded from the UUID, rebuilt after a reload with its charges carried over), energy, ability power at
  its level (`HeroRules.abilityPower`) and the running move. `combat/HeroAbilityGoal` (priority 2, MOVE and LOOK) asks
  the selector every 5 ticks while the hero fights and runs no move, against its current target whoever that is,
  with a `Sense` that `HeroSenses` fills (one reused sense; `Sense.clear` is new); the move then advances in the
  hero's own tick, so its timing never depends on when the goal selector runs. Between moves the goal holds nothing
  and the soldiers' basic attack goals space and strike. The fight's opening gap starts when `inFight` first turns
  on.
- **The timing seam for A5.** `logic/hero/MovePlan.Plan` is a move's schedule in ticks from its start: the wind-up
  (the first hit), every hit tick, the end of the active part and of the recovery. Hits by form: melee, bolt, nova,
  cone and line with `hits` every `hit_interval`; a combo's strikes spread over the active part, or each after the
  previous strike's `active` plus its own `windup`; a chain one jump every 3 ticks; every other form one release at
  the end of the wind-up (the projectile, the zone or the motion resolves later). `HeroCombat.planFor` hands in the
  move's authored frames today; A5 hands in the timing file's frames at the hero's speed there, sets `DATA_ACTION` in
  `Run.begin`, and sends its interrupts through `stopRun` (a wind-up stopped gives half the cooldown back, v1 10.3).
  `STATE_WINDUP` is on during the wind-up; a smoke ring every 5 ticks marks where a nova, zone or leap will land (the
  decals are A6's). A target that dies, goes or moves more than 2 blocks past the move's range during the wind-up
  calls it off with the charge kept. After a use that landed, a followup replaces the recovery (6.3).
- **Forms** (`hero/HeroForms`, `logic/hero/FormGeometry`): enemies are exactly whom the hero's side fights (hostile
  factions' NPCs and heroes with pacts respected, enemy players, hostile monsters, its own target), allies its side
  with the hero itself; animals, villagers, golems, armour stands and display copies are never touched; an area
  takes at most 16 receivers, nearest first. Melee and combo strikes reach 3.5 blocks to the target's box plus the
  strike's splash; a bolt and a chain's first link need sight; a cone reads its angle, a line stops at the first solid
  block, a volley fans 10 degrees apart; a leap is a physical arc (`FormGeometry.leap` on F5's `LeapSolver`) that
  lands a block short of its target and strikes on landing or after 40 ticks; a dash pushes 0.5 s along the ground,
  hits each enemy within 1.5 blocks of its path once and stops at a wall or a drop; the hero takes no fall damage
  from its own leap or hop. `hero/entity/HeroProjectile` (a `ThrowableItemProjectile` showing the look's vanilla item;
  custom models are A9's) passes through allies, changes no block, drops nothing and is not saved; a volley's
  projectiles share one use. `hero/entity/HeroZone` (invisible, server particles, not saved) pulses
  `1 + duration / interval` times; at most two zones a hero.
- **Effects and amounts** (`hero/HeroEffects`, `logic/hero/EffectRouting`): each effect goes where its `to` says (the
  form's receivers, the hero once per use, the allies inside a nova, zone or leap, the allies around the hero, the most
  hurt ally), only to receivers its recipient class allows, rolled by its `chance` and checked by its `if` for each
  receiver (`Conditions`, the receiver as the sense's target; `target_behind` reads both ways, kit-gaps "A3
  decisions"). An Amount reads the hero's attack power, health and armour and the receiver's health, missing health
  and marks at hit time, x ability power x `heroes.powerScale`. `logic/hero/UseLedger` holds what one use lands on
  one receiver (all its hits, pulses and projectiles) to the slot cap x ability power x power scale x 1.5 for gear x
  1.5 for a pulsing zone or 1.25 for a combo (v1 16.1, 6.5, 6.14 rule 3), whoever the receiver is.
- **Damage** (`hero/HeroDamage`): v1 6.3's factors (the attacker's `damage_dealt`, the element against what the target
  is, a hero target's resistance, the target's `damage_taken`, clamped 0-2), at most `heroes.maxHit`, with
  `factionclash:hero_<element>` and `_dot` damage types (`data/factionclash/damage_type`, scaling `never`; tagged
  `is_fire`, `bypasses_armor` for arcane, `no_knockback` and `bypasses_shield` for DoTs, `witch_resistant_to`,
  `wither_immune_to`; death messages in `en_us.json`). A hero hit clears the target's hurt cooldown and puts it back
  after, so hero hits are never swallowed and never swallow a soldier's sword. `hero/HeroCombatEvents` gives vanilla
  damage the same stats (`damage_dealt`, `damage_taken`, a hero target's resistance to the element vanilla's damage
  maps to) and `healing_received` on every heal. The per-hit boss cap on a hero target is A6a's.
- **Statuses** (`hero/HeroStatus` over `logic/hero/StatusBook`): marks with stacks (shared ids, one "full" report),
  damage over time once a second summed per hero and target and held to `heroes.maxDotPerSecond`, stat modifiers
  (attribute modifiers, one per stat, the sum held to v1 13.3's range; the others read by the rules), the shield
  (absorption with its room on `MAX_ABSORPTION`, at most 60% of max health, replaced by a stronger one, gone on
  time), the freeze hold and cleanse. A data attachment (`factionclash:hero_status`) on any living entity, saved with
  expiries as game times, stats put back on load; at most 32 statuses an entity; every carrier is ticked once a
  server tick with nothing allocated while nothing is due.
- **Energy** (v1 14): 5 for a landed basic hit (a melee blow, a thrown projectile, or the hero's own arrow, tagged
  when shot), a skill's `energy_gain`, damage taken and kills (A1b), healing and shielding (the hero counts as an
  ally), the `energy` effect; each x the ultimate's `charge_rate` x the `energy_gain` stat. The ultimate needs 100
  and spends all of it. A basic hit also applies the basic attack's `on_hit` and its `every_nth_hit` finisher.
- **Thrown heroes** (`combat/HeroThrowGoal`, priority 3): a `thrown` hero no longer fights in melee
  (`FactionNpc.fightsInMelee`); it throws its projectile look every `interval` within 14 blocks and in sight, backs
  away like an archer inside half its `keep_distance` and closes in through `Pursuit.approachRanged`.
- **Saving.** The hero's `FcHero` tag keeps every move's charges and recharge as game times, the last ultimate and the
  basic-hit count beside the energy; a recharge saved longer than the move's cooldown now comes no later than that.
  The fight's own clock, the running move, projectiles and zones are not saved (a reload ends the fight).
- **Equal treatment.** Nothing in the ability core reads whether a target is a player beyond vanilla's own creative
  and spectator rule (`CombatRules.isEnemyPlayer`, 6.14 rule 6); the GameTests' mock player is a NeoForge
  `FakePlayer` in survival, a member of an enemy or allied faction (`makeMockServerPlayerInLevel` gives a creative
  player, which is never an enemy). The GameTest server is a dedicated server, so the common side loads no client
  class.
- **Elsewhere**: `MoveSelector.runnable` (only moves the engine runs in full are picked, answered or followed;
  `EffectRouting.runs`), `snapshot`/`restore`, `cutCooldowns` and `readyAt`; `FactionNpc.markAttacked` and
